const support = @import("support");

test "built_ins/list/immutable/with/original" {
    try support.check(.values, .{ .input = .{.items = &.{0, 1, 2}, .index = 1, .count = 0, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 3}, .later = .{.items = &.{0, 1, 2}, .index = 1, .count = 0, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 1}, .expected = &.{0, 3, 2}, .following = &.{0, 1, 2} });
}

test "built_ins/list/immutable/with/allocation_failures" {
    try support.check(.allocations, .{ .input = .{.items = &.{0, 1, 2}, .index = 1, .count = 0, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 3}, .later = .{.items = &.{0, 1, 2}, .index = 1, .count = 0, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 1}, .expected = &.{0, 3, 2}, .following = &.{0, 1, 2} });
}
